sampling）\n3. kl001_r4 的 best eval（step 90）rel_f1=0.211，仍低于 0.30 阈值——lr=5e-5 系列整体 FAIL 已确认（D027）\n\n## 可查的历史\n前 29 轮 handoff 在 `task_context/history/agent/loop_*.md`，如有疑问可 Read 回查。\n完整聊天归档可用 ### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
- **服务器**：rtx5090-4x（RTX 5090 32GB × 4），账号 root
- **工作目录**：`/root/autodl-tmp/freige`
- **Python**：`/root/miniconda3/bin/python`（conda base 环境，不是 venv）
- **环境激活**：`source /root/miniconda3/bin/activate && cd /root/autodl-tmp && python ...`
- **Eval 脚本**：`/root/autodl-tmp/scripts/eval_lr5e6_ckpt*.sh`
- **活跃进程**：
  - PID 90080 — mvp2_grpo_ced_lr5e6_r1（cuda:0，训练，step ~68/200）
  - PID 101842 — mvp2_eval_lr5e6_r1_ckpt40_r2（cuda:2，ckpt-40 eval）
  - PID 108025 — mvp2_eval_lr5e6_r1_ckpt60（cuda:1，ckpt-60 eval）
  - PID 104979 — mvp2_eval_sft_baseline（cuda:3，SFT baseline eval）
- **活跃 Cron**：3 个（监控上述 PID 90080 / 101842 / 104979），ckpt60 的 cron 未见（可能需要补注册）
- **W&B**：项目 `faith-evidence-ie`

### 3. 📊 当前状态
**刚做完**：lr5e6 vs kl001_r4 的轨迹对比分析（结论写入 memory.md）；ckpt-20 eval negative（rel_f1=0.0077）；kill 了 rp_r2 训练线和 ckpt-60_r2 重复实验。

**4 卡全忙**：
| GPU | 实验 | 状态 |
|-----|------|------|
| cuda:0 | mvp2_grpo_ced_lr5e6_r1 | 训练 step ~68/200，预计 3-4h 完成 |
| cuda:1 | mvp2_eval_lr5e6_r1_ckpt60 | ckpt-60 eval 运行中 |
| cuda:2 | mvp2_eval_lr5e6_r1_ckpt40_r2 | ckpt-40 eval 运行中 |
| cuda:3 | mvp2_eval_sft_baseline | SFT baseline eval 运行中 |

**等待回调**：3 个 cron 监控中（PID 90080/101842/104979）。ckpt-60 eval（PID 108025）可能没有 cron 监控——接手后需确认并补注册。

**Worker 池**：0 个 running worker——因为 4 卡全忙且无非 GPU 任务待执行，但接手后应考虑是否有文献/代码准备类 CPU 任务可以并行。

### 4. 📋 待办（条件触发式）
- **ckpt-40 eval 完成（PID 101842 退出）** → 读结果；rel_f1 > 0.30 → P0 PASS 该 checkpoint，汇报 Director；rel_f1 < 0.05 → lr=5e-6 基本判定失败趋势
- **ckpt-60 eval 完成（PID 108025 退出）** → 同上。注意确认 cron 是否存在，不存在则手动 spawn worker 读结果
- **SFT baseline eval 完成（PID 104979 退出）** → 读 rel_f1，作为 apple-to-apple 对比基线。之前 SFT eval r3 用了不同 eval path
- **训练 step 80 到达** → 准备 ckpt-80 eval（mvp2_eval_lr5e6_r1_ckpt80 已 PREPARING），分配空出的 GPU 提交
- **全 trajectory 出齐（ckpt-20/40/60/80 + SFT baseline 全部 DONE）** → 做 MVP-2 最终判定：任一 > 0.30 = PASS；全 < 0.30 但上升 = 延长训练；全 < 0.30 且 plateau = FAIL → pivot 到 DPO/rejection sampling（contingency 见 ROADMAP）
- **训练完成（PID 90080 退出，step 200）** → 做最终 checkpoint eval；汇报 Director 全 trajectory

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
**核心问题**：CED reward 信号在 MVP-1 验证有效（margin 0.4827, acc 81.5%），但 GRPO+QLoRA 训练方法论失败——lr=5e-5 全系列 mode collapse/遗忘，lr=5e-6 前 80 步几乎不学习（f1_reward 在 0-0.04 随机波动，88% reward 来自 format hacking）。

**当前赌注**：lr=5e-6 的 ckpt-40/60 是否出现迟到的上升拐点。kl001_r4（lr=5e-5）的 step 40 才出现拐点，所以 lr=5e-6 可能更晚。但 memory.md 的分析结论偏悲观：lr5e6 到 step 80 仍无拐点迹象。

**最易判断错的事**：
1. 不要把 ckpt-40 eval 的 mvp2_eval_lr5e6_r1_ckpt40（DONE/failed，PID auto-fail）和 mvp2_eval_lr5e6_r1_ckpt40_r2（RUNNING，重新追踪同一 PID 101842）混淆——后者才是真正在跑的
2. 最优 LR 在 1e-5 到 2e-5 之间（memory.md 结论），5e-5 太高 5e-6 太低，但当前没有中间值的实验。如果全 trajectory FAIL，下一步不是再调 LR，而是换训练方法论（DPO/rejection sampling）
3. kl001_r4 的 best eval（step 90）rel_f1=0.211，仍低于 0.30 阈值——lr=5e-5 系列整体 FAIL 已确认（D027）

    "addedLines": [
      "- claude: Catch-all for any task that doesn't fit a more specific agent. FleetView's default when no agent name is typed. (Tools: *)",
      "- Explore: Fast read-only search agent for locating code. Use it to find files by pattern (eg. \"src/components/**/*.tsx\"), grep for symbols or keywords (eg. \"API endpoints\"), or answer \"where is X defined / which files reference Y.\" Do NOT use it for code review, design-doc auditing, cross-file consistency checks, or open-ended analysis — it reads excerpts rather than whole files and will miss content past its read window. When calling, specify search breadth: \"quick\" for a single targeted lookup, \"medium\" for moderate exploration, or \"very thorough\" to search across multiple locations and naming conventions. (Tools: All tools except Agent, ExitPlanMode, Edit, Write, NotebookEdit)",
      "- general-purpose: General-purpose agent for researching complex questions, searching for code, and executing multi-step tasks. When you are searching for a keyword or file and are not confident that you will find the right match in the first few tries use this agent to perform the search for you. (Tools: *)",
      "- Plan: Software architect agent for designing implementation plans. Use this when you need to plan the implementation strategy for a task. Returns step-by-step plans, identifies critical files, and considers architectural trade-offs. (Tools: All tools except Agent, ExitPlanMode, Edit, Write, NotebookEdit)",
      "- statusline-setup: Use this agent to configure the user's Claude Code status line setting. (Tools: Read, Edit)"
    ],

{
  "parentUuid": "35ffd22c-30bb-4621-bd92-d6789bf43320",
  "isSidechain": false,
  "attachment": {
    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

KL early stopping correctly triggered, model saved but no useful training occurred.\\\\n\\\\nRoot cause: single-GPU training without --gradient_accumulation_steps (effective batch=1) → extremely noisy gradients → policy diverged from reference in 5 steps. Combined with QLoRA merge rounding amplifying initial policy-reference gap.\\\\n\\\\nKey metrics at step 5: loss=269987, kl=4.4M, entropy=0.084 (near 0), format_reward=0.10, f1_reward=0.05, ced_reward=0.15, grad_norm=0.16. EXIT_CODE=129 is artifact of KL early stop exit path.\\\\n\\\\nerror_type: hyperparameter | can_auto_fix: false\\\\nFix options: (1) add --gradient_accumulation_steps 16 for effective batch ~16; (2) reduce lr from 5e-5 to 5e-6; (3) increase kl_coef from 0.05 to 0.5; (4) lr warmup steps\\\"}, \\\"mvp2_grpo_ced_r2\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T10:58:11.663Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T11:31:49.724Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"killed\\\"}, \\\"mvp2_grpo_ced_r1\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T08:02:41.858Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T10:37:23.856Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"killed\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"Mode collapse diagnosed at step 80/189 (F1=0.001, format reward hacking). Root causes and fixes applied to grpo_trainer.py:\\\\n\\\\n(A) Checkpoint: save_strategy was \\\\\\\"epoch\\\\\\\" → now supports --save_steps/--save_total_limit CLI args, defaults to steps-based saving. Validated: checkpoint-1/2 saved in dry-run.\\\\n\\\\n(B) Reward weighting: all 3 rewards (format/f1/ced) were equally summed by TRL → added WeightedRewardFn wrapper + --format_reward_weight/--f1_reward_weight/--ced_reward_weight CLI args. Dry-run confirmed weighting works (format raw=0.1 × w=0.3 → 0.03).\\\\n\\\\n(C) Clip ratio: num_iterations=1 (default) meant single pass, ratio≡1, clipping never activated → added --num_iterations CLI arg (set to 2 in r2). Also added --max_steps, --kl_coef (beta). Dry-run clip_ratio=0 expected (zero reward variance → zero advantage → no update needed).\\\\n\\\\n(D) Added --save_total_limit CLI arg (previously hardcoded to 2, now configurable).\\\\n\\\\nDry-run validated: 2 steps completed, checkpoints saved, no OOM with num_iterations=2 on 4xRTX5090. completions/clipped_ratio=1.0 (all hit max_completion_length=1024) — expected for cold-start without warmup.\\\"}, \\\"mvp2_grpo_ced\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T07:10:31.165Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T08:00:02.874Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": <details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01AfGwCpDia3Cfkz6PMTjJoM`</summary>

```text
1	---
2	name: resource-saturation
3	description: "当你准备向 Director 汇报前、收到 cron/worker 回调、审批通过准备开跑、怀疑 Worker 池不够饱和时,立即使用本 skill。四部分:A) GPU 分配率(分配了没跑=失职) B) GPU 效率 C) 非 GPU 任务并行(Worker 池<2=失职) D) 汇报必须带 [资源] 行。"
4	always: false
5	roles:
6	  - director
7	  - agent
8	---
9	
10	# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）
11	
12	## A. GPU 分配率检查
13	
14	调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:
15	- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号
16	- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）
17	- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️
18	
19	任何"分配了但没跑"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:
20	- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备
21	- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）
22	- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报"暂无可铺实验"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作
23	
24	**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。
25	
26	## B. GPU 效率检查
27	
28	调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:
29	- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务
30	- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）
31	- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优
32	
33	## C. 非 GPU 任务并行
34	
35	GPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:
36	- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint
37	- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置
38	- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索
39	- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合
40	- 论文章节起草
41	- 代码 review / refactor
42	
43	**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。
44	
45	## D. 汇报格式
46	
47	向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:
48	
49	    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)
50	
```

{
  "task_type": "research:contingency-literature",
  "project": "faith-evidence-ie",
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：调研 DPO/Rejection Sampling 用于结构化生成任务的方法\n\n### 背景\n项目 faith-evidence-ie 正在用 GRPO+QLoRA 训练 LLM 做文档级关系抽取（DocRE）。GRPO 训练遇到严重问题：\n- lr=5e-5 导致灾难性遗忘 + mode collapse\n- lr=5e-6 前 20 步完全不学习（format compliance 仅 39.9%，rel_f1=0.0077）\n- CED（对比证据判别）reward 信号本身有效（MVP-1 margin=0.4827, accuracy=81.5%），但 GRPO 训练方法论失败\n\n如果 lr=5e-6 的后续 checkpoint（ckpt-40/60/80）仍然 FAIL，需要 pivot 到其他训练方法。当前首选 contingency：DPO 或 Rejection Sampling。\n\n### 调研目标\n\n1. **DPO + CED scoring**：搜索 2024-2026 年论文，找以下方向：\n   - DPO 用于结构化生成/信息抽取任务的案例\n   - 离线 reward 信号构建偏好对的方法（类似我们可以用 CED 打分构建 preferred/rejected pairs）\n   - DPO 在 QLoRA/4-bit 量化下的稳定性报告\n\n2. **Rejection Sampling Fine-Tuning (RFT)**：\n   - RFT 用于结构化输出任务的案例\n   - 如何用 CED 作为过滤器（生成多个输出，CED 打分选最好的做 SFT）\n   - 样本效率：需要生成多少个才能获得足够好的训练数据\n\n3. **其他替代方案**：\n   - RLHF 之外的 alignment 方法用于 IE 任务\n   - 迭代式 SFT（self-training / iterative refinement）\n   - KTO (Kahneman-Tversky Optimization) 用于只有 binary 反馈的场景\n\n### 搜索关键词建议\n- \"DPO structured generation\"\n- \"rejection sampling fine-tuning information extraction\"\n- \"offline preference optimization QLoRA\"\n- \"DPO relation extraction\"\n- \"iterative self-training NLP\"\n\n### 产出格式\n写入项目文件 `contingency_methods_survey.md`，包含：\n- 每种方法的 1-2 段总结\n- 关键论文引用（标题、年份、arXiv ID）\n- 适用于我们场景的具体实施建议\n- 推荐排序（基于我们的场景：CED reward 可离线计算、QLoRA 约束、结构化输出）\n\n### 工具\n用 WebSearch 搜索论文和相关资料。",
  "tool_groups": [
    "knowledge"
  ],
  "tools": [
    "WebSearch"
  ]
}
